Dependency and Status Model

Volume 1 supplies tenant context, public identifiers, audit, idempotency and durable messaging. Volumes 2 to 9 consume those
controls. Volume 10 governs release evidence. Volumes 11 to 15 add operational, interoperability, engagement, configuration and
DHIS2 capabilities without weakening the core controls.

Status dimension  Allowed values          Meaning

Functional        Draft; Approved         Requirements authority
Engineering       Candidate; Verified
                                          Candidate is designed; Verified is
                                          executed with evidence

Supplement        Proposed; Incorporated  Whether supplemental scope is
                                          accepted into the controlled baseline

Production        Not Approved; Approved  Deployment authority

Unified Traceability Contract

Every implemented requirement shall map to: SRD requirement ID, EDD volume, design component, package/class, database
object, API or event, test case, approval evidence and release identifier. Missing links block Verified status.

Consolidated Review Gates

� Resolve the SRD and EDD technology-baseline discrepancy.
� Confirm every model has a matching migration and every table has an owning model or explicit reason.
� Approve one atomic permission catalogue across all volumes.
� Close or formally defer every blocking gap with an owner and acceptance test.
� Run clean, upgrade, rollback, concurrency, load, privacy, tenant-isolation and security suites.
� Generate signed CI evidence and source-package checksums.

Part II. Architecture, Transactions and Failure Behaviour

Request Pipeline

1. Authenticate through host middleware.
2. Resolve tenant header.
3. Compare tenant to authenticated identity.
4. Resolve or generate correlation ID.
5. Create immutable ClinicalRequestContext.
6. Apply authorization policy for the requested Clinical action.
7. Apply idempotency middleware for state-changing commands.
8. Execute one application action in a database transaction.
9. Write audit/outbox records within the declared transaction where required.
10. Return standard envelope and correlation header.

Transaction Rules

� No remote HTTP call is made while holding a database transaction unless an approved exception is documented.
� Domain state and the corresponding outbox message commit atomically.
� Idempotency ownership is acquired under a row lock.
� Audit records required to authorize or evidence a high-risk action commit with the action.
� Retries use bounded database transaction attempts and stable identities.

Failure Catalogue

Code                          HTTP  Retryable                                        Meaning

CLN_CTX_TENANT_REQUIRED       400   No                                               Tenant context missing.
                                    No                                               Authenticated identity and requested tenant differ.
CLN_CTX_TENANT_MISMATCH       403   No                                               State-changing route requires key.
                                    No                                               Key used with different request hash.
CLN_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED  400   Yes                                              Equivalent request is still executing.
                                    Conditional                                      Required authority cannot be proven.
CLN_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED    409   Conditional                                      Mandatory audit evidence cannot be stored.
                                    Yes                                              Durable event could not be written.
CLN_IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS   409

CLN_AUTH_CONTEXT_UNAVAILABLE  503

CLN_AUDIT_UNAVAILABLE         503

CLN_OUTBOX_FAILED             503

Security Rules

� Tenant is never accepted solely from route or request body.
� Internal numeric IDs are never returned by package APIs.
� Correlation IDs are validated as ULIDs before propagation.
� Audit and event payloads use allow-listed fields.
� Secrets exist only in environment or secret manager.
� Cross-tenant lookup returns concealed not-found behaviour in domain repositories.
� All write routes require server-side authorization plus idempotency.

# KashTre Clinical Foundation v6.1.0
Status: Pick-and-drop candidate. Repository execution is required before VERIFIED classification.
## Install
1. Copy this package under `packages/kashtre/clinical-foundation`.
2. Add a Composer path repository and require `kashtre/clinical-foundation:@dev`.
3. Publish configuration: `php artisan vendor:publish --tag=clinical-foundation-config`.
4. Configure `CLINICAL_*` environment values.
5. Run `php artisan migrate`.
6. Run `composer test` inside the package.
## Required host contract
The authenticated user model must expose `public_id` and `tenant_id`. If KashTre uses different accessors, implement an approved
ActorIdentity adapter before verification.

<?php

namespace KashTre\Clinical\Foundation\Infrastructure;

use KashTre\Clinical\Foundation\Contracts\TenantResolver;
use KashTre\Clinical\Foundation\ValueObjects\TenantId;
final class HeaderTenantResolver implements TenantResolver
{

      public function resolve(Request $request): TenantId
      {

             $header=(string) config('clinical-foundation.tenant_header');
             $value=$request->header($header);
             if (!is_string($value) || $value==='') throw new BadRequestHttpException('Tenant context is required.');
             return new TenantId($value);
      }

      public function __toString(): string { return $this->value; }
}

Document Authority and Non-Negotiable Rule

The Observation Plan creates obligations and due work. It never creates, copies or fabricates patient Observation values. One
eligible authoritative Observation may satisfy multiple applicable requirements while every source remains traceable.

Area                               Controlled decision

Sources                            Baseline, client-space profile, form, order, protocol, care plan,
                                   medication monitoring, patient-specific and transfer continuity.

Consolidation                      Same target and compatible context become one obligation.

Strictness                         Shortest compatible interval wins; source provenance is
                                   retained.

History                            Past due events are not rewritten by prospective recalculation.

Satisfaction                       Tenant, patient, encounter, target, time window, status, quality
                                   and context must match.

Transfer                           Unresolved due work moves once with original lateness
                                   evidence.

Rendering                          Separate consumer; failure cannot roll back committed
                                   Observation or satisfaction.

Engineering Design

Recalculation pipeline                                    Purpose
                                                          View plan and explanation
� Lock encounter planning scope                           Request recalculation
� Gather versioned active sources                         Create hold/exemption/override
� Normalize to RequirementCandidate                       Resolve not obtained or defer
� Evaluate applicability using explicit facts             Resolve planning conflicts
� Group by semantic key                                   Transfer responsibility
� Select stricter compatible cadence
� Persist plan generation, obligations and source links
� Materialize future due events
� Write audit and outbox facts

Satisfaction pipeline

� Select unresolved candidate due events under lock
� Match tenant, patient and encounter
� Match CDE or Observation Group target
� Match occurrence window and approved late-entry policy
� Match status, quality, context and source restrictions
� Create idempotent satisfaction link
� Mark event SATISFIED
� Publish satisfaction event

Transfer pipeline

� Confirm movement
� Create unique transfer lineage
� Freeze unresolved due/overdue work
� Recalculate destination plan
� Continue non-location sources
� End source-client-space-only requirements
� Create receiving work once
� Preserve scheduled and overdue timestamps
� Publish transfer event

Mandatory Tests

� Three sources for BP produce one due event
� Hourly order overrides compatible four-hourly form cadence
� Source ending does not end obligation required elsewhere
� Repeated recalculation produces no duplicate generation
� Repeated scheduler run creates no duplicate occurrence
� One Observation satisfies multiple obligations where eligible
� Entered-in-error Observation reverses satisfaction
� Transfer moves overdue work once without resetting lateness
� Cancelled transfer has no planning effect
� Cross-tenant source is rejected
� Missing applicability fact enters safe failure
� Rendering outage does not affect plan or Observation commit

<?php
declare(strict_types=1);
namespace KashTre\Clinical\ObservationPlan\Services;
use KashTre\Clinical\ObservationPlan\Data\RequirementCandidate;
final class RequirementConsolidator{

 public function consolidate(array $candidates):array{$groups=[];foreach($candidates as $c){if(!$c instanceof
RequirementCandidate)throw new \InvalidArgumentException('RequirementCandidate required.');$key=hash('sha256',implode('|',[$c-
>targetType->value,$c->targetPublicId,$c->targetVersion,json_encode($c-
>clinicalContext,JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR)]));$groups[$key][]=$c;}$out=[];foreach($groups as $key=>$items){usort($items,fn($a,$b)=>($a-
>intervalMinutes??PHP_INT_MAX)<=>($b-
>intervalMinutes??PHP_INT_MAX));$winner=$items[0];$out[]=['semantic_key'=>$key,'winner'=>$winner,'sources'=>array_map(fn($x)=>$x-
>sourcePublicId,$items)];}return $out;}
}

Authority and Safety Rule

Clinical Observations are authoritative patient-specific event records. Original values, original units, normalized values, status,
effective time, performer, source and provenance are preserved. No guessed unit, incompatible conversion, stale range, wrong-
patient association or destructive correction is permitted.

Entity                     Purpose                         Critical invariant

CdeDefinition              CDE Definition                  Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

CdeVersion                 CDE Version                     Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

CdeAllowedUnit             CDE AllowedUnit                 Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

CdeValueSetItem            CDE ValueSetItem                Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

CdeLimitPolicy             CDE LimitPolicy                 Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

Observation                Observation                     Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

ObservationComponent       Observation Component           Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

ObservationGroup           Observation Group               Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

ObservationProvenance      Observation Provenance          Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

ObservationCorrection      Observation Correction          Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

DevicePayload              DevicePayload                   Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

DeviceMapping              DeviceMapping                   Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

ObservationReconciliation  Observation Reconciliation      Tenant-scoped, ULID-addressed and
                                                           historically preserved

Observation Lifecycle                         Meaning
 State                                        Stored before validation where policy permits
 REGISTERED                                   Available with visible preliminary status
 PRELIMINARY                                  Authoritative completed result
 FINAL                                        Supplemented without replacing meaning
 AMENDED                                      Superseded by linked replacement
 CORRECTED                                    Workflow cancelled with evidence
 CANCELLED                                    Excluded from ordinary use, audit retained
 ENTERED_IN_ERROR

Capture Channels                              Commit rule

Engineering Design

CDE governance

Stable definition plus immutable versions

Data type and semantic meaning versioned

Allowed input and display units reference Main Unit Engine IDs

Value sets are versioned

Physiological warning and block limits are effective-dated

Activation requires review and approval

Observation validation

Patient and encounter exact match

CDE version active at occurrence

Value kind matches CDE type

Unit required and allowed for quantities

Conversion uses Shared Unit Engine

Original and normalized values both persist

Physiological limit outcome explicit

Performer, source and effective time required

Device processing

Idempotent message identity

Raw payload hash preserved

No automatic chart commit by default

Mapping uses device plus source code

Stale patient/bed association blocks commit

Quarantine rather than guessing

Correction

No destructive edit of final observation

Replacement observation is a complete event

Original status changes to CORRECTED

Correction reason and linkage persist

Plan satisfaction, score and alert reconciliation triggered

Permissions

Permission                                                      Purpose

clinical.cde_config.manage                                      Create and maintain CDE drafts

clinical.cde_config.approve                                     Approve and activate versions

clinical.observation.view                                       View authorized observations

clinical.observation.create                                     Record manual observation

clinical.observation.validate                                   Validate staged observation

clinical.observation.override_limit                             Override eligible warning

clinical.observation.correct                                    Create correction

clinical.observation.mark_entered_in_error                      Invalidate erroneous observation

clinical.observation.device_reconcile                           Resolve device payload exception

                                                                          View detailed provenance

Mandatory Tests
Quantity without unit is blocked
Disallowed unit is blocked
Unit Engine failure never relabels original value
Physiological block prevents commit
Warning requires visible outcome
Device retry creates one payload
Wrong-patient device association is quarantined
Final observation cannot be edited in place
Correction preserves original and replacement
Entered-in-error reverses plan satisfaction
One valid observation satisfies all eligible plan obligations
Cross-tenant CDE or patient reference is rejected

                  DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                           KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Authority and Governing Flow

Form definitions and clinical context determine required observations. Volume 2 converts those requirements into due work. Volume
3 stores each authoritative Observation once. Volume 4 renders every applicable form from those Observations and never creates an
independently editable duplicate value.

Form definition + client-space/profile/patient activation
  -> observation requirements
  -> Effective Observation Plan
  -> nurse captures Observation once
  -> continuous form population
  -> read-only, longitudinal and printable rendering

               DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                        KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Engineering Design

Form governance

Stable code and public ID

Immutable approved versions

Unique item keys

CDE references rather than copied semantics

Separate semantic and layout changes

Reference PDF or Word is evidence, not executable form

Approval before activation

Client-space profile

Versioned facility/client-space profile

Default forms assigned by activation mode

Applicability rules explicit

Prospective changes only

Overflow location inherits governing profile

Patient-specific Add Form does not alter defaults

Continuous population

Query qualifying Observations by patient, encounter, CDE, time, status and context

One Observation may populate several forms

Source observation IDs retained per rendered item

Missing positions show pending/due/overdue/not applicable

Correction marks snapshot stale and regenerates

Rendering failure never rolls back Observation

Add Form

Filter approved eligible catalogue

Warn on equivalent active form

Create activation against exact version

Contribute requirements to Volume 2

Prepopulate existing qualifying Observations

Retain historical form after completion

Transfer

Preserve prior form activations and history

End source-space-only requirements prospectively

Activate destination defaults

Continue ordered/protocol/patient-specific forms

Never delete Labour Care Guide after transfer

Permission                                                 Purpose

clinical.form_definition.create                            Create draft

clinical.form_definition.update                            Edit draft

clinical.form_definition.review                            Review version

             DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
clinical.form_definition.approve   KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15
clinical.form_definition.activate             Approve semantic and layout version
clinical.form_assignment.manage               Make version usable
clinical.patient_form.add                     Assign to client-space profile
clinical.patient_form.discontinue             Add to one patient
clinical.form_render.view                     End prospective applicability
clinical.form_render.print                    View rendered form
clinical.form_reconcile                       Generate controlled print
                                              Resolve rendering or binding failures

Mandatory Tests
Two forms requiring BP create one due task through Volume 2
One BP Observation populates both forms
Adding form prepopulates existing valid observations
Adding form does not change client-space defaults
Unapproved form version cannot activate
Duplicate item key is rejected
Rendering failure leaves Observation intact
Corrected Observation regenerates every dependent snapshot
Transfer preserves historical Labour form
Print output identifies version, patient, encounter, timezone and generation time
Cross-tenant form cannot activate
Reference asset cannot execute active logic

Authority and Safety Rule

A score is a reproducible derived clinical event. Every score must retain the exact score-definition version, input Observation IDs and
versions, selection rules, component points, aggregate result, algorithm version, calculation time and triggered response rules. A
missing input is never silently replaced with zero unless the approved score definition explicitly requires that behaviour.

Authoritative Observations
 -> score input selection
 -> deterministic component scoring
 -> aggregate score and calculated Observation
 -> governed trigger rules
 -> alert routing
 -> acknowledgement
 -> assessment and action
 -> escalation if overdue
 -> resolution with evidence

Engineering Design

Score governance

Stable definition plus immutable approved versions

Each input references a CDE version

Selection window and method are explicit

Component and aggregate rules are data, not scattered code

Output is written as a calculated Observation

Licensed or externally governed score material is not reconstructed without approval

Calculation

Use only eligible final or policy-permitted inputs

Normalize quantities before scoring

Reject absent required input

Retain all input Observation IDs

Calculate component points deterministically

Hash inputs and algorithm for idempotency

Correction invalidates dependent score and triggers recalculation

Deterioration alerts

Trigger from governed rule only

Route to eligible current care context

Delivery is not acknowledgement

Acknowledgement is not assessment

Assessment is not action

Action is not resolution

Escalate when configured stage due

Preserve every delivery and escalation attempt

Safe failure

Scoring engine outage does not alter source Observations

Notification outage retains triggered alert

Unknown recipient enters reconciliation

Duplicate source event cannot duplicate alert

Stale or corrected input visibly invalidates prior score

Permissions

Permission                                                         Purpose

clinical.score_definition.manage                                   Create and maintain score drafts

clinical.score_definition.approve                                  Approve and activate score version

clinical.score.calculate                                           Calculate score

clinical.score.view                                                View score and components

clinical.alert.view                                                View applicable alert

clinical.alert.acknowledge                                         Acknowledge exact alert version

clinical.alert.assess                                              Record assessment

clinical.alert.action                                              Record clinical action

                                             Configure escalation routes

                                             Resolve calculation or alert exception

Mandatory Tests
Missing required input blocks score
Input outside selection window is not used
Correct normalized unit is used
Same inputs and version produce one score run
Different score version produces distinct run
Component sum and trigger rules are reproducible
Correction invalidates dependent score
Notification failure preserves alert
Delivery does not mark acknowledged
Acknowledgement does not mark resolved
Overdue escalation creates next routing action once
Cross-tenant alert is concealed
Expired client-space assignment cannot acknowledge ordinary alert
Historical score remains reproducible after definition deprecation

namespace KashTre\Clinical\Scoring\Services;
final class DeterministicRuleEvaluator{public function matches(string $operator,string $value,?string $lower,?string $upper,array
$allowed=[]):bool{return
match($operator){'LT'=>(float)$value<(float)$lower,'LTE'=>(float)$value<=(float)$lower,'EQ'=>$value===$lower,'GTE'=>(float)$value>
=(float)$lower,'GT'=>(float)$value>(float)$lower,'BETWEEN'=>(float)$value>=(float)$lower&&(float)$value<=(float)$upper,'IN'=>in_arr
ay($value,$allowed,true),default=>throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Unsupported rule operator')};}}

                                  KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Authority and Safety Rule

The Medication Administration event is the Clinical truth that a medicine was administered or not administered. Pharmacy remains
authoritative for dispensing and product verification. Inventory remains authoritative for physical stock consequences. Main remains
authoritative for Approved Pool, coverage and financial consequences. A valid Clinical administration is never deleted because
Inventory or Main is unavailable.

Medication order -> eMAR opportunity -> patient/product verification -> administration commit -> draft Consumption Session -> add
incidental supplies or confirm none -> Inventory acceptance -> Main financial evaluation

        DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                        KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Engineering Design

Medication orders and eMAR

One active order version generates deterministic dose opportunities

Schedule timezone and local intent retained

Order hold or discontinuation ends future opportunities prospectively

eMAR displays due, overdue, administered and not-administered states

Administration safety

Verify patient, order, product, dose, route, time and performer

Separate clinical dose from physical package quantity

Capture lot and expiry where available

Record start and completion time separately

Prevent second administration against same opportunity

Support partial dose and multiple products

Waste and infusions

Separate administered quantity from waste

Require witness where configured

Breakage and contamination are not patient administration

Infusion pause, resume, rate change and bag change are distinct events

PRN administration creates reassessment work

Consumption

Draft session created only after valid administration commit

Medication product is prepopulated

Incidental supplies can be scanned or selected

User confirms all items or no additional items

One grouped idempotent session sent to Inventory

Inventory and Main processing times never replace physical-use time

Failure enters reconciliation without invalidating administration

Permissions

Permission                                                         Purpose

clinical.medication_order.create                                   Create order

clinical.medication_order.sign                                     Authorize order

clinical.medication_administer                                     Commit administration

clinical.medication_waste.record                                   Record waste

clinical.medication_waste.witness                                  Witness controlled waste

clinical.infusion.manage                                           Manage infusion events

clinical.consumption.confirm                                       Confirm session

clinical.consumption.defer                                         Save for reconciliation

clinical.consumption.correct                                       Correct physical-use lines

clinical.medication.reconcile                                      Resolve medication exception

Mandatory Tests
                  DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                       KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Wrong patient blocks administration

Wrong product or lot blocks administration

Duplicate submit creates one administration

Clinical dose and physical quantity remain separate

Partial dose and waste remain separate

Inventory outage preserves administration and session

Approved Pool decision is not requested from nurse

PRN administration creates reassessment work

Correction preserves original administration

Same session retry does not duplicate Inventory consumption

Transfer does not duplicate due opportunities

Cross-tenant medication order is concealed

                  DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                   KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Authority and Safety Rule

LIMS, Imaging and other diagnostic modules remain authoritative for report production and authorization. Clinical stores a versioned
projection for review, acknowledgement, follow-up and patient-care continuity. Clinical never silently changes the source report, and
ambiguous patient or order matching is quarantined rather than guessed.

Source report -> inbox idempotency -> patient/order match -> projection -> critical routing -> clinician review -> follow-up task ->
patient communication -> completion -> correction propagation

                                                                    and provenance retained

                                                                    and provenance retained

        DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                          KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Engineering Design

Ingestion

Idempotent source system, report ID and version

Preserve issued, effective and received times separately

Retain source payload hash

Quarantine ambiguous or unmatched records

Store atomic result items and attachments without becoming source authority

Review

Review exact source version

Viewing is not review

Review is not acknowledgement of a critical result

Action-required review creates a due follow-up task

Correction creates a new review obligation

Critical results

Trigger once per critical item

Route to eligible current clinical context

Delivery does not equal acknowledgement

Acknowledgement records actor and time

Unacknowledged critical results escalate

Resolution requires recorded assessment and action

Post-discharge continuity

Results arriving after discharge remain visible

Outstanding follow-up remains owned

Patient communication records channel, attempt and outcome

Encounter closure does not erase pending result work

Permissions

Permission                                                  Purpose

clinical.result.view                                        View matched result

clinical.result.review                                      Record review

clinical.result.acknowledge_critical                        Acknowledge critical result

clinical.result.followup.create                             Create follow-up

clinical.result.followup.complete                           Complete follow-up with evidence

clinical.result.patient_communicate                         Record communication

clinical.result.quarantine.resolve                          Resolve match exception

clinical.result.reconcile                                   Resolve ingestion/correction exception

Mandatory Tests
Duplicate source version creates one projection
Different corrected version creates distinct projection
Ambiguous patient match quarantines
Critical delivery does not acknowledge
Review of old version does not review correction

Action-required review creates one task
Post-discharge result remains actionable
Notification outage preserves critical record
Cross-tenant result is concealed
Attachment hash mismatch is quarantined
Source cancellation remains historically visible
Follow-up cannot complete without outcome evidence

                                  KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Authority and Safety Rule

A transition is a governed clinical event, not a change of screen location. Discharge, transfer and referral cannot erase active
observations, overdue work, medication responsibilities, critical results or follow-up obligations. Readiness is evaluated from
authoritative services, warnings are explicit and blocking conditions require resolution or a separately authorized exception.

Initiate transition -> readiness snapshot -> resolve blockers -> authorize -> handover -> movement/discharge/referral -> transfer
outstanding work -> acknowledge receipt -> follow-up -> attested summary

Engineering Design

Readiness

Open medication administration and infusion checks

Medication reconciliation

Outstanding diagnostic results and critical acknowledgements

Observation Plan due and overdue work

Active alerts and unresolved tasks

Destination and follow-up arrangements

                                     KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Authority and Assurance Rule

Clinical assurance must prove who accessed or changed clinical information, under which authority, for what purpose, and with
what outcome. Break glass is time-limited and reviewable. Consent and confidentiality decisions are recorded. Downtime records
preserve the real clinical occurrence time and are reconciled without silent duplication or overwriting.

Authorization + consent decision -> permitted action or denial -> immutable audit event -> monitoring and assurance checks ->
incident or break-glass review when indicated -> corrective action and closure

RetentionDisposition          clinical_retention_dispositions     Tenant-scoped, immutable evidence or
                                                                  controlled lifecycle

        DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                          KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Engineering Design

Consent and confidentiality

Evaluate patient, actor, action, purpose and resource context

Record permit, deny, conditional or indeterminate result

Do not treat user authentication as consent

Preserve policy and consent references

Fail closed for restricted writes when authority cannot be proven

Break glass

Require reason, justification and requested actions

Limit scope and duration

Audit start, use, expiry and revocation

Display active emergency access

Require independent retrospective review

Audit and provenance

Record occurred and recorded times separately

Hash-chain security-relevant audit events

Retain actor, patient, entity, outcome and correlation evidence

Verify chain integrity

Keep operational audit separate from clinical record provenance

Downtime

Declare scoped downtime episode

Capture offline reference and actual occurrence time

Prevent duplicate import

Reconcile against authoritative records

Escalate conflicts and missing records

Close only when required reconciliations complete

Incidents and assurance

Record patient safety, privacy, security and operational incidents

Run deterministic assurance checks

Retain findings and corrective actions

Controlled exports require authorization, expiry and download audit

Retention disposition must never bypass legal hold

Permissions

Permission                                                           Purpose

clinical.consent.decision.view                                       View decision evidence

clinical.break_glass.request                                         Request emergency access

clinical.break_glass.review                                          Review emergency episode

clinical.audit.search                                                Search authorized audit

clinical.audit.export                                                Request controlled export

clinical.incident.create                                             Report incident

                                                                   Reconcile offline records

Mandatory Tests
Denied consent decision blocks restricted action
Indeterminate decision fails closed for write
Break glass without justification is rejected
Expired break glass cannot authorize access
Every break-glass use is auditable
Audit hash-chain tampering is detected
Offline duplicate is not imported twice
Downtime occurrence time is not replaced by entry time
Reconciliation conflict is escalated
Export expires and download is audited
Cross-tenant audit access is concealed
Legal hold blocks retention disposition

run(iterable $checks,array $context):array{$results=[];$overall='PASS';foreach($checks
as$c){$r=$c($context);$results[]=$r;if(($r['status']??'FAIL')==='FAIL')$overall='FAIL';elseif(($r['status']??'WARNING')==='WARNING'&&
$overall==='PASS')$overall='WARNING';}return['overall'=>$overall,'results'=>$results,'fingerprint'=>hash('sha256',json_encode($res
ults,JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR))];}}

Authority and Release Rule

No Clinical release becomes production-approved because source code exists or a pipeline is green. Release authorization requires
controlled traceability, reproducible artifacts, verified tests, migration rehearsal, security evidence, clinical acceptance, observable
deployment, rollback readiness and post-deployment verification.

Requirements -> implementation references -> tests -> signed artifacts and SBOM -> release gates -> multi-party approval ->
deployment -> canary observation -> post-deployment verification -> live or rollback -> evidence retention

Engineering Design

Traceability

Every controlled SRD requirement maps to implementation and tests

Artifact manifest identifies exact files and hashes

Source commit, build identity and dependency lock are retained

Untraced safety requirement blocks release

Quality gates

Unit, integration, contract, concurrency and end-to-end evidence

Tenant isolation and authorization negatives

Clinical gold scenarios across Volumes 1 to 9

Accessibility and supported-browser verification

Performance, queue, outage and recovery verification

Security gates

SBOM required

Dependency and secret scanning

Static and dynamic security testing

Threat-model and abuse-case review

OWASP ASVS evidence version-pinned

Unresolved critical or high defect blocks authorization

Database and deployment

Forward and rollback migration rehearsal

Backup and restore verification

Expand-contract deployment for incompatible schema change

Canary or controlled cohort before broad release

Observable steps with immutable timestamps

Post-deployment

Verify identity, tenancy, authorization, events, queues and integrations

Run clinical smoke scenarios

Observe error, latency, backlog and reconciliation signals

Rollback decision is explicit and authorized

Document residual defects and corrective work

Required Approval Roles

Role                                                              Decision focus

Clinical Product Authority                                        Functional and clinical acceptance

Engineering Authority                                             Implementation, migration and rollback

Security and Privacy Authority                                    Security, privacy and consent controls

Operations Authority                                              Deployment, monitoring and recovery

Data/Integration Authority                                        Cross-module contracts and reconciliation

Mandatory Tests

Untraced requirement blocks READY

Changed artifact hash invalidates evidence
                  DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                          KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Missing SBOM blocks authorization

Failed tenant-isolation test cannot be waived locally

Migration rehearsal records forward and rollback outcomes

Duplicate deployment request is idempotent

Canary failure prevents LIVE state

Rollback preserves audit and clinical records

Queue backlog alert appears during canary

Post-deployment smoke test covers observation, medication, result and transition workflows

Approval actor cannot approve outside assigned authority

Production artifact exactly matches authorized manifest

<?php
declare(strict_types=1);namespace KashTre\Clinical\ReleaseAssurance\Services;use
KashTre\Clinical\ReleaseAssurance\Contracts\ReleaseGateContributor;final class ReleaseGateEngine{public function
__construct(private iterable $contributors){}public function evaluate(string $tenantId,string $releaseId,array
$context):array{$results=[];$overall='PASS';foreach($this->contributors as$c){if(!$c instanceof
ReleaseGateContributor)continue;foreach($c-
>evaluate($tenantId,$releaseId,$context)as$r){$results[]=$r;if(($r['outcome']??'FAIL')==='FAIL')$overall='FAIL';elseif(($r['outcome']??
'PASS')==='WARNING'&&$overall==='PASS')$overall='WARNING';}}return['overall'=>$overall,'results'=>$results,'fingerprint'=>hash('s
ha256',json_encode($results,JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR))];}}

Scope Position and Governing Rule

The governing SRD states that Phases 1 to 10 form the complete functional baseline. Volume 11 is therefore supplemental. It defines
the post-release operating model needed to sustain adoption, competency, support, improvement, benefits realization and
controlled AI assistance without changing the approved clinical source-of-truth boundaries.

Production release -> supported operation -> issue and knowledge management -> training and competency -> adoption monitoring -
> governed optimization -> benefits review -> safe change control

                                                               KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Engineering Design

Support and knowledge

Single operating issue and support-case intake

Severity and patient-safety impact separated

Known error and workaround articles are versioned

Resolution links to release, configuration or training action

No support action silently edits clinical records

Training and competency

Versioned curricula by function and workflow

Assignments use authoritative user and employment identity

Completion is not the same as competency

High-risk functionality may require current competency

Expiry and revocation are effective-dated

Adoption and benefits

Use auditable workflow telemetry, not surveillance of individuals

Define each metric, denominator, threshold and limitation

Separate adoption, quality, safety, user experience and outcome measures

Drill down by authorized organizational scope

Low adoption triggers investigation, not punitive inference

Optimization

Every proposed change has problem statement and safety impact

Pilot before broad adoption where risk warrants

Compare baseline and pilot evidence

Route semantic changes through the proper CDE, form, score or workflow governance

Rollback ineffective or unsafe optimization

AI governance

Clinical consumes AI only through the Shared AI Gateway

Use case must be registered, risk-rated and active

Send minimum necessary context

Output remains draft or advisory until human review

Model and gateway references are retained

Rejected outputs and monitoring findings inform suspension

Prohibited use cases cannot execute

Permissions

Permission                                                         Purpose

clinical.operations.dashboard.view                                 View authorized operating posture

clinical.support.manage                                            Triage and resolve support issues

clinical.knowledge.publish                                         Approve knowledge article

clinical.training.assign                                           Assign curriculum

clinical.competency.assess                                         Record competency decision

clinical.adoption.view                                             View adoption measures

clinical.optimization.propose  KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15
clinical.optimization.approve             Submit improvement proposal
clinical.ai.use                           Authorize pilot or adoption
clinical.ai.review                        Request approved AI assistance
clinical.ai.govern                        Accept or reject AI output
                                          Manage AI use cases and findings

Mandatory Tests
Inactive AI use case is rejected
Prohibited AI use cannot execute
AI output cannot become a clinical record without review
Accepted output stores reviewer and accepted content
Direct provider credential path is unavailable
Expired competency blocks configured high-risk action
Training completion alone does not create competency
Metric without denominator is rejected
Cross-tenant telemetry is concealed
Optimization cannot bypass CDE or form governance
Support resolution cannot rewrite clinical event
Knowledge supersession preserves historical article version

<?php
declare(strict_types=1);namespace KashTre\Clinical\Operations\Services;final class AdoptionHealthEvaluator{public function
evaluate(array $metricDefinitions,array $values):array{$results=[];$overall='HEALTHY';foreach($metricDefinitions
as$d){$value=$values[$d['code']]??null;$signal='NOT_MEASURED';if($value!==null){$signal=(float)$value<(float)$d['action_threshol
d']?'ACTION_REQUIRED':((float)$value<(float)$d['watch_threshold']?'WATCH':'HEALTHY');}$results[]=['code'=>$d['code'],'value'=>$v
alue,'signal'=>$signal];if($signal==='ACTION_REQUIRED')$overall='ACTION_REQUIRED';elseif($signal==='WATCH'&&$overall==='HE
ALTHY')$overall='WATCH';}return['overall'=>$overall,'results'=>$results];}}

Scope Position and Governing Rule

Volume 12 is supplemental. It exposes governed representations of authoritative Clinical data without creating a second clinical
source of truth. Every exchange records purpose, authorization, scope, profile version, de-identification mode, artifact hash, delivery
evidence and expiry.

Authoritative clinical records -> purpose and authorization -> cohort/scope resolution -> FHIR mapping -> terminology validation ->
de-identification where required -> encrypted artifact or event notification -> delivery evidence -> reconciliation and expiry

Engineering Design

FHIR exchange

Versioned mapping profiles

Stable public identifiers

Capability-aware endpoints

No direct database exposure

Validation errors enter reconciliation

Source corrections produce new exchange versions

Subscriptions

Governed SubscriptionTopic catalogue

Authorized endpoint and filters

Idempotent notification event reference

Retry with bounded attempts

Suspend repeatedly failing or unsafe endpoints

Notification does not itself disclose more than configured content

Bulk data

Asynchronous job lifecycle

Immutable NDJSON artifact hashes

Encrypted object storage

Expiring download authorization

Manifest and record counts

No direct identifiers by default for secondary use

Cohorts and measures

Versioned cohort criteria

Membership snapshots preserve evaluation time

Approved measure logic and implementation version

Input fingerprint for reproducibility

Individual, subject-list or summary outputs as configured

Public health and research

Purpose and legal/consent authority recorded

Minimum necessary scope

De-identification policy explicit

Research protocol and approval reference required

No re-identification path in Clinical

Submission acceptance or rejection retained

Permissions

Permission                                                          Purpose

clinical.exchange.request                                           Request authorized exchange

clinical.exchange.generate                                          Generate artifact

clinical.exchange.deliver                                           Deliver approved artifact

clinical.subscription.manage                                        Manage topic-based notification

Mandatory Tests
Denied purpose blocks exchange
Cross-tenant record cannot enter artifact
Source correction produces new mapped version
Artifact hash verifies downloaded content
Expired job cannot be downloaded
Subscription retry is idempotent
Failed endpoint is suspended at configured boundary
De-identified export contains no configured direct identifiers
Cohort membership is reproducible
Measure run repeats from identical fingerprint
Research request without protocol is rejected
Public-health rejection is retained and correctable

KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE
CODE-READY ENGINEERING DESIGN DOCUMENT

Volume 13 Authoritative Correction Notice

This correction supersedes inherited Volume 12 exchange, cohort, measure, public-health and research entity names
wherever they appear inside Volume 13. Those inherited names are retained only as historical draft evidence and shall not be
implemented as the Volume 13 domain model.

Corrected Volume 13 Entity Register     Purpose
                                        Authorized patient-facing clinical view
 Entity                                 Verified, purpose-limited and effective-dated representative
 PatientPortalProjection                authority
                                        Versioned patient-facing questionnaire
 ProxyRelationship                      Patient or proxy assignment and due state
                                        Partial or completed structured response
 QuestionnaireDefinition                Secure patient-care conversation context
 QuestionnaireAssignment                Versioned message and delivery evidence
 PatientReportedResponse                Approved remote monitoring program assignment
 CommunicationThread                    Effective-dated device-to-patient association
 PatientMessage                         Measured, transmitted and received values with provenance
 RemoteMonitoringEnrollment             Clinical review and escalation obligation
 RemoteDeviceAssociation                Remote encounter continuity and outcome
 RemoteMeasurement                      Identity, device, delivery or mapping exception
 RemoteReviewTask
 VirtualCareSession
 EngagementReconciliation

Corrected Volume 13 Package Boundary

� Package: kashtre/clinical-engagement.
� Namespace: KashTre\Clinical\Engagement.
� Tables use the clinical_engagement, clinical_patient_message, clinical_questionnaire and clinical_remote_monitoring families.
� Patient-entered information remains distinguishable from clinician-entered information.
� A threshold breach creates review work and never an autonomous diagnosis.
� Proxy access requires current relationship, purpose, scope, consent and confidentiality clearance.

Volume 13 Required Engineering Completion

� Replace inherited interoperability source listings with engagement-specific models, migrations, actions, policies, APIs and
      tests.

� Add proxy expiry and revocation tests.
� Add questionnaire cardinality, type and correction tests.
� Add secure-message delivery, failure escalation and sensitive-preview tests.
� Add duplicate and out-of-order remote-measurement tests.
� Add virtual-care encounter and provenance tests.

PATIENT ENGAGEMENT, REMOTE CARE AND PATIENT-REPORTED DATA

Portal Access, Proxy Relationships, Questionnaires, Messaging, Remote Monitoring and Escalation

Scope Position and Governing Rule

Volume 13 is supplemental. It exposes governed representations of authoritative Clinical data without creating a second clinical
source of truth. Every exchange records purpose, authorization, scope, profile version, de-identification mode, artifact hash, delivery
evidence and expiry.

Patient or proxy authentication -> relationship and consent check -> questionnaire, message or device submission -> validation ->
Clinical projection -> review or escalation -> acknowledgement -> follow-up and closure

Engineering Design

Patient and proxy access

Patient-facing views use authorized projections

Proxy authority is purpose- and time-bounded

Sensitive-content rules are enforced server-side

Patient portal never exposes Clinical tables directly

Identity or relationship ambiguity enters reconciliation

Corrections remain visible as new versions

Secure communications

Threaded patient-care communication

Patient, proxy and care-team recipient verification

Idempotent message delivery reference

Bounded delivery retry

Escalate failed urgent communication

Notification preview contains minimum necessary content

Patient-reported data

Versioned questionnaires and response assignments

Complete and partial response states

Answer validation and provenance

Patient-entered data remains distinguishable from clinician-entered data

Approved response extraction into Observations

No automatic clinical conclusion from a response

Remote monitoring

Registered device and patient association

Measured, transmitted and received times retained

Quality and plausibility flags

Duplicate and out-of-order handling

Threshold breach creates review work, not autonomous diagnosis

Virtual-care continuity

Remote-service assignment and encounter relationship required

Consent and communication preferences respected

Urgent symptoms use configured escalation instructions

Video or teleconsultation provider integration remains external

Clinical record stores encounter outcome and provenance

Failed contact attempts and follow-up retained

Permissions

Permission                                                       Purpose

clinical.patient_portal.view                                     View authorized patient-facing record

clinical.proxy.manage                                            Manage verified proxy relationship

clinical.questionnaire.assign                                    Assign patient questionnaire

clinical.patient_message.respond                                 Respond to patient message

clinical.remote_monitoring.enrol         KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15
clinical.remote_monitoring.review                   Enrol remote monitoring
clinical.virtual_care.document                      Review transmitted measurement
clinical.patient_reported.review                    Document virtual-care outcome
clinical.patient_communication.escalate             Review patient-reported data
clinical.engagement.reconcile                       Escalate urgent communication
                                                    Resolve identity, device or delivery exception

Mandatory Tests
Unverified proxy cannot access patient content
Expired proxy authority is rejected
Patient response remains distinguishable from clinician observation
Questionnaire validation rejects invalid answer type
Duplicate device transmission is idempotent
Out-of-order measurement preserves measured time
Threshold breach creates review work once
Urgent message delivery failure escalates
Cross-tenant portal access is concealed
Notification preview excludes sensitive content
Virtual-care outcome retains encounter provenance
Correction preserves prior patient-reported version

Scope Position and Governing Rule

Volume 14 is supplemental. It governs clinical configuration as controlled executable content rather than informal settings. CDEs,
forms, score definitions, alert policies, questionnaires, terminology bindings, translations and tenant overrides are versioned,
validated, approved, promoted and reversible.

Author content -> resolve dependencies -> validate semantics and terminology -> clinical review -> translation and accessibility
review -> immutable package -> environment promotion -> drift monitoring -> rollback or supersession

Engineering Design

Controlled content catalogue

Stable content code and immutable versions

Explicit dependency graph

Payload hashes

Effective dates and supersession

No in-place production editing

Validation and approval

Schema, semantic, terminology and reference validation

Safety-impact and backward-compatibility evidence

Separation of author and approver

Failed validation blocks promotion

Warnings require explicit disposition

Promotion and rollback

Immutable promotion manifest

Pre-promotion environment snapshot

Ordered dependency-aware activation

Idempotent package promotion

Post-promotion verification

Rollback restores controlled snapshot without deleting history

Localization

Source and target locale retained

Linguistic review separated from clinical review

Clinical terms cannot be machine-published without review

Fallback language explicit

Right-to-left and pluralization behavior testable

Accessibility

Target standard is version-pinned

Keyboard, focus, labeling, contrast and error presentation tests

Accessible authentication and redundant-entry checks

Failure blocks publication for patient-facing or clinical workflow content

Tenant overrides and drift

Override is narrow, effective-dated and attributable

Base version remains identifiable

Safety-critical constraints cannot be weakened locally

Environment drift is detected by manifest comparison

Unexpected production content enters reconciliation

Permissions

Permission                                                        Purpose

clinical.content.create                                           Create controlled content version

clinical.content.validate                                         Run validation

               DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
clinical.content.approve               KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15
clinical.content.translate                        Approve content
clinical.content.translation_review               Create translation
clinical.content.accessibility_review             Approve clinical translation
clinical.content.promote                          Approve accessibility evidence
clinical.content.rollback                         Promote approved package
clinical.content.override                         Execute authorized rollback
clinical.content.drift.review                     Manage tenant override
                                                  Resolve configuration drift

Mandatory Tests
In-place edit of active content is impossible
Missing dependency blocks validation
Failed terminology binding blocks approval
Author cannot satisfy independent approval alone
Machine translation cannot become active without clinical review
Accessibility failure blocks patient-facing publication
Promotion order respects dependencies
Repeated promotion is idempotent
Rollback restores prior manifest
Tenant override cannot weaken safety constraint
Production drift is detected
Supersession preserves historical rendering

KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE
CODE-READY ENGINEERING DESIGN DOCUMENT

Volume 15 Consolidated Engineering Expansion

The following section is normative for the DHIS2 integration candidate and expands the compact original Volume 15 section.

DHIS2 Component Architecture

� Instance Registry stores tenant, environment, base URI reference, supported DHIS2 version and capability snapshot without
      embedding credentials.

� Metadata Snapshot captures organisation units, data elements, category combinations, option sets, datasets, programs, stages
      and tracked-entity types with hash and retrieval time.

� Mapping Registry versions KashTre-to-DHIS2 mappings and prevents ad hoc production edits.
� Payload Builder creates Aggregate, Event or Tracker payloads from approved source events and reporting rules.
� Delivery Orchestrator performs validation-only or commit imports, records idempotency identity and supports synchronous or

      asynchronous completion.
� Import Reconciler parses summaries and object reports, links every failure to source correlation, and controls correction and

      resubmission.
� Drift Monitor compares active mappings with target metadata and suspends affected delivery when required metadata changes.

DHIS2 Data Objects                      Purpose
                                        Target endpoint, environment and supported capability
 Entity                                 Secret-manager reference only
 Dhis2Instance                          Hashed target metadata view
 Dhis2CredentialReference               Facility/client-space to organisation-unit mapping
 Dhis2MetadataSnapshot                  KashTre measure to DHIS2 data element
 Dhis2OrganisationUnitMapping           Category option combination mapping
 Dhis2DataElementMapping                Dataset and reporting-period contract
 Dhis2CategoryMapping                   Event or Tracker program mapping
 Dhis2DatasetMapping                    Program-stage and data-value mapping
 Dhis2ProgramMapping                    Approved identifier and attribute policy
 Dhis2ProgramStageMapping               Aggregate, Event or Tracker submission identity
 Dhis2TrackedEntityMapping              Source-to-target object correlation
 Dhis2SubmissionBatch                   Request/response and retry evidence
 Dhis2SubmissionObject                  Overall import outcome
 Dhis2DeliveryAttempt                   Object-level conflict or error
 Dhis2ImportSummary                     Detected target metadata change
 Dhis2ImportObjectReport                Correction, resubmission and closure work
 Dhis2MetadataDrift
 Dhis2ReconciliationCase

DHIS2 Processing Rules

� Aggregate submissions contain no patient identifier.
� Tracker or Event disclosure requires approved public-health purpose and minimum-necessary mapping.
� The idempotency key is derived from tenant, target instance, mapping version, source public ID and source version.
� Validation-only imports create no target data.

                  DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                                            KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15
� Asynchronous jobs retain job identity and are polled with bounded retry.
� Partial or rejected imports never become accepted silently.
� Reporting-period correction preserves earlier submission evidence.
� Metadata drift blocks only affected mappings and creates an actionable case.

� Credentials are obtained at execution time from the approved secrets platform and are never logged.

DHIS2 APIs and Workspaces Required

Area                                Required candidate

API                                 Instance, metadata snapshot, mapping, validation,
                                    submission, retry and reconciliation controllers

Queue                               Metadata refresh, payload creation, delivery, polling and
                                    reconciliation workers

Scheduler                           Reporting-period generation, backlog retry and drift monitoring

Vue                                 Instance registry, mapping studio, submission monitor and
                                    reconciliation workspace

Observability                       Delivery latency, acceptance ratio, rejection reasons, backlog
                                    age, retry count and drift alerts

Tests                               Contract, privacy, tenant isolation, idempotency, metadata
                                    drift, outage, correction and resubmission suites

SUPPLEMENTAL VOLUME 15

DHIS2 INTEGRATION, PUBLIC-HEALTH REPORTING AND SURVEILLANCE

Aggregate Data Sets, Event and Tracker Programs, Metadata Mapping, Delivery and Reconciliation

           DRAFT FOR ENGINEERING REVIEW | PICK-AND-DROP CANDIDATE | NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION
                                                            KASHTRE CLINICAL MODULE | CONSOLIDATED EDD v6.1 | VOLUMES 1-15

Scope and Authority

KashTre remains authoritative for patient care, encounters, clinical observations and source events. DHIS2 is the destination
authority for configured reporting datasets and public-health programs.

Volume 15 is supplemental to the Phase 1 to 10 baseline and depends on Volumes 1 to 14.

Integration Architecture

Authoritative KashTre event -> approved reporting rule -> metadata and organisation-unit mapping -> validation or dry run ->
aggregate, Event or Tracker payload -> DHIS2 Web API -> import summary -> reconciliation and resubmission.

Aggregate Reporting

Map dataset, period, organisation unit, data element and category option combination.

Exclude patient identifiers from aggregate data.

Support correction and resubmission without deleting prior evidence.

Event and Tracker Programs

Use Event programs for standalone reportable events.

Use Tracker for approved longitudinal program records using tracked entities, enrollments, events and relationships.

Preserve source public ID, event version, occurred time, correlation ID and mapping version.

Metadata Governance

Snapshot DHIS2 metadata and identifiers.

Use effective-dated mappings for organisation units, data elements, option sets, programs and stages.

Suspend affected delivery when required target metadata drifts.

Delivery and Reconciliation

Support validation before commit.

Use idempotency keys and bounded retry.

Retain import summaries and object reports.

Create actionable reconciliation cases for rejected or partially accepted objects.

Privacy and Security

Credentials remain in the platform secrets service, not Clinical source code.

Tracker and Event disclosure requires an approved purpose and minimum-necessary mapping.

Every transmission and retry is audited.

Mandatory Acceptance Tests

Missing organisation-unit mapping blocks delivery.

Unknown data element blocks aggregate submission.

Duplicate source event does not duplicate a Tracker event.

Dry-run validation creates no target data.

Rejected object maps back to the source correlation ID.

Aggregate payload contains no patient identifier.

Cross-tenant mapping is concealed.

Metadata drift suspends affected rules.

Blocking Gaps

Confirm target DHIS2 instances and versions.

Approve datasets, Event programs and Tracker programs.

Approve Sierra Leone organisation-unit and metadata mappings.

Approve identity and pseudonymisation policy.

Standards and Product References

DHIS2 metadata identifier schemes support stable UIDs, codes and unique metadata attributes for mapping external systems. The
current Tracker API uses /api/tracker and query endpoints for tracked entities, enrollments, events and relationships.

Repository verification is required before production approval.
